// RiskManager.js - Advanced risk management and dynamic position sizing
// Manages risk based on volatility, drawdown, win/loss streaks, and market conditions

/**
 * Adaptive Risk Manager for OGZ Prime trading system
 * Controls position sizing, drawdown protection, and recovery mode
 */
class RiskManager {
  /**
   * Create a new Risk Manager
   * @param {Object} config - Configuration options
   */
  constructor(config = {}) {
    // Default configuration with optimal risk settings
    this.config = {
      // Base risk percentage per trade (of account)
      baseRiskPercent: 2.0,
      
      // Maximum account percentage to risk per trade
      maxPositionSizePercent: 5.0,
      
      // Minimum account percentage to risk per trade (floor)
      minPositionSizePercent: 0.5,
      
      // Maximum drawdown allowed before disabling trading
      maxDrawdownPercent: 15,
      
      // Drawdown threshold to enter recovery mode
      recoveryThreshold: 10,
      
      // Position size adjustment for consecutive losses
      consecutiveLossReduction: 0.2,  // Reduce by 20% after each loss
      
      // Position size adjustment for consecutive wins
      winStreakIncrease: 0.1,         // Increase by 10% after each win
      
      // Maximum consecutive win increase (cap)
      maxWinStreakMultiplier: 2.0,    // Never more than double the size
      
      // Enable volatility-based position sizing
      volatilityScaling: true,
      
      // Volatility adjustment factor
      volatilityFactor: 1.0,
      
      // Reduction in high volatility conditions
      highVolatilityReduction: 0.5,   // 50% size in high volatility
      
      // Required trades to exit recovery mode
      tradesRequiredToExitRecovery: 5,
      
      // Higher confidence requirement during recovery
      recoveryConfidenceMultiplier: 1.5,
      
      // Risk reduction for reversal/counter-trend trades
      counterTrendRiskReduction: 0.3, // 30% smaller positions against trend
      
      // Daily loss limit as percent of account
      dailyLossLimitPercent: 5.0,
      
      // Weekly loss limit as percent of account
      weeklyLossLimitPercent: 10.0,
      
      // Monthly loss limit as percent of account
      monthlyLossLimitPercent: 15.0,
      
      // Extra log level
      verboseLogging: false,
      
      // Merged with user configuration
      ...config
    };
    
    // Initialize state
    this.reset();
  }
  
  /**
   * Reset risk manager state
   */
  reset() {
    this.state = {
      // Account state tracking
      initialBalance: 0,
      peakBalance: 0,
      currentDrawdown: 0,
      maxDrawdown: 0,
      
      // Consecutive trade tracking
      consecutiveLosses: 0,
      consecutiveWins: 0,
      
      // Recovery mode state
      inRecoveryMode: false,
      tradesSinceDrawdown: 0,
      drawdownDetectedAt: null,
      
      // Daily/Weekly/Monthly tracking
      dailyStats: this.createPeriodStats(),
      weeklyStats: this.createPeriodStats(),
      monthlyStats: this.createPeriodStats(),
      
      // Trading permission flags
      tradingEnabled: true,
      tradingDisabledReason: null,
      
      // History
      tradeLog: [],
      drawdownEvents: []
    };
  }
  
  /**
   * Create stats tracker for a time period
   * @returns {Object} Period stats object
   */
  createPeriodStats() {
    return {
      startBalance: 0,
      currentBalance: 0,
      trades: 0,
      wins: 0,
      losses: 0,
      pnl: 0,
      maxProfit: 0,
      maxLoss: 0,
      startDate: new Date(),
      lastUpdated: new Date(),
      breachedLimit: false
    };
  }
  
  /**
   * Initialize with current account balance
   * @param {number} balance - Current account balance
   */
  initialize(balance) {
    if (!balance || balance <= 0) {
      throw new Error('Invalid balance provided to RiskManager');
    }
    
    this.state.initialBalance = balance;
    this.state.peakBalance = balance;
    
    // Initialize period stats
    this.state.dailyStats.startBalance = balance;
    this.state.dailyStats.currentBalance = balance;
    this.state.weeklyStats.startBalance = balance;
    this.state.weeklyStats.currentBalance = balance;
    this.state.monthlyStats.startBalance = balance;
    this.state.monthlyStats.currentBalance = balance;
    
    this.log(`Risk Manager initialized with balance: $${balance.toFixed(2)}`);
    return true;
  }
  
  /**
   * Check if trading is allowed based on risk limits
   * @returns {Object} Status including whether trading is allowed
   */
  canTrade() {
    // Already disabled
    if (!this.state.tradingEnabled) {
      return {
        allowed: false,
        reason: this.state.tradingDisabledReason
      };
    }
    
    // Check drawdown limits
    if (this.state.currentDrawdown >= this.config.maxDrawdownPercent) {
      this.disableTrading(`Maximum drawdown reached (${this.state.currentDrawdown.toFixed(1)}%)`);
      return {
        allowed: false,
        reason: this.state.tradingDisabledReason
      };
    }
    
    // Check daily loss limit
    if (this.state.dailyStats.breachedLimit) {
      this.disableTrading(`Daily loss limit reached (${this.config.dailyLossLimitPercent}%)`);
      return {
        allowed: false,
        reason: this.state.tradingDisabledReason
      };
    }
    
    // Check weekly loss limit
    if (this.state.weeklyStats.breachedLimit) {
      this.disableTrading(`Weekly loss limit reached (${this.config.weeklyLossLimitPercent}%)`);
      return {
        allowed: false,
        reason: this.state.tradingDisabledReason
      };
    }
    
    // Check monthly loss limit
    if (this.state.monthlyStats.breachedLimit) {
      this.disableTrading(`Monthly loss limit reached (${this.config.monthlyLossLimitPercent}%)`);
      return {
        allowed: false,
        reason: this.state.tradingDisabledReason
      };
    }
    
    return {
      allowed: true
    };
  }
  
  /**
   * Disable trading with reason
   * @param {string} reason - Reason for disabling
   */
  disableTrading(reason) {
    this.state.tradingEnabled = false;
    this.state.tradingDisabledReason = reason;
    this.log(`⛔ Trading disabled: ${reason}`, 'warning');
    return false;
  }
  
  /**
   * Enable trading
   */
  enableTrading() {
    this.state.tradingEnabled = true;
    this.state.tradingDisabledReason = null;
    this.log('✅ Trading enabled', 'info');
    return true;
  }
  
  /**
   * Reset daily statistics
   */
  resetDailyStats(balance) {
    this.state.dailyStats = this.createPeriodStats();
    this.state.dailyStats.startBalance = balance;
    this.state.dailyStats.currentBalance = balance;
    this.log('📅 Daily stats reset', 'info');
  }
  
  /**
   * Reset weekly statistics
   */
  resetWeeklyStats(balance) {
    this.state.weeklyStats = this.createPeriodStats();
    this.state.weeklyStats.startBalance = balance;
    this.state.weeklyStats.currentBalance = balance;
    this.log('📅 Weekly stats reset', 'info');
  }
  
  /**
   * Reset monthly statistics
   */
  resetMonthlyStats(balance) {
    this.state.monthlyStats = this.createPeriodStats();
    this.state.monthlyStats.startBalance = balance;
    this.state.monthlyStats.currentBalance = balance;
    this.log('📅 Monthly stats reset', 'info');
  }
  
  /**
   * Calculate position size based on current risk profile
   * @param {number} balance - Current account balance
   * @param {number} price - Current instrument price
   * @param {number} confidence - Signal confidence (0-1)
   * @param {Object} marketConditions - Current market conditions
   * @returns {Object} Position size information
   */
  calculatePositionSize(balance, price, confidence = 0.5, marketConditions = {}) {
    // Validate inputs
    if (!balance || balance <= 0 || !price || price <= 0) {
      this.log('Invalid inputs for position sizing', 'error');
      return {
        size: 0,
        sizeUsd: 0,
        riskPercent: 0,
        allowed: false,
        reason: 'Invalid inputs'
      };
    }
    
    // Check trading permission
    const tradingStatus = this.canTrade();
    if (!tradingStatus.allowed) {
      return {
        size: 0,
        sizeUsd: 0,
        riskPercent: 0,
        allowed: false,
        reason: tradingStatus.reason
      };
    }
    
    // Confidence must be scaled to reasonable range
    confidence = Math.max(0.1, Math.min(1, confidence));
    
    // Extract market conditions
    const {
      volatility = 0.01,           // Default to medium volatility
      trend = 'sideways',          // Default to sideways market
      isCounterTrend = false,      // Is this a counter-trend trade?
      atrValue = null,             // ATR value if available
    } = marketConditions;
    
    // Start with base risk percentage
    let riskPercent = this.config.baseRiskPercent;
    
    // Apply risk adjustments based on win/loss streaks
    riskPercent = this.applyStreakAdjustments(riskPercent);
    
    // Apply volatility adjustments
    riskPercent = this.applyVolatilityAdjustments(riskPercent, volatility);
    
    // Apply confidence scaling
    riskPercent = this.applyConfidenceScaling(riskPercent, confidence);
    
    // Apply counter-trend adjustment if needed
    if (isCounterTrend) {
      riskPercent *= (1 - this.config.counterTrendRiskReduction);
      this.log(`Applied counter-trend reduction: ${riskPercent.toFixed(2)}%`, 'debug');
    }
    
    // Apply recovery mode adjustment if active
    if (this.state.inRecoveryMode) {
      riskPercent *= 0.5; // 50% size during recovery
      this.log(`Applied recovery mode reduction: ${riskPercent.toFixed(2)}%`, 'debug');
    }
    
    // Enforce minimum and maximum risk
    riskPercent = Math.max(this.config.minPositionSizePercent, 
                          Math.min(this.config.maxPositionSizePercent, riskPercent));
    
    // Calculate dollar amount to risk
    const riskAmountUsd = balance * (riskPercent / 100);
    
    // Calculate position size in units
    const positionSize = riskAmountUsd / price;
    
    return {
      size: positionSize,
      sizeUsd: riskAmountUsd,
      riskPercent: riskPercent,
      allowed: true,
      adjustments: {
        streakFactor: this.getStreakFactor(),
        volatilityFactor: this.getVolatilityFactor(volatility),
        confidenceFactor: confidence,
        inRecoveryMode: this.state.inRecoveryMode
      }
    };
  }
  
  /**
   * Apply win/loss streak adjustments to position size
   * @param {number} baseRiskPercent - Starting risk percentage
   * @returns {number} Adjusted risk percentage
   */
  applyStreakAdjustments(baseRiskPercent) {
    // Decrease size after consecutive losses
    if (this.state.consecutiveLosses > 0) {
      const reduction = Math.min(0.8, this.state.consecutiveLosses * this.config.consecutiveLossReduction);
      baseRiskPercent *= (1 - reduction);
      this.log(`Size reduced due to ${this.state.consecutiveLosses} consecutive losses`, 'debug');
    }
    
    // Increase size after consecutive wins
    if (this.state.consecutiveWins > 0) {
      const increase = Math.min(
        this.config.maxWinStreakMultiplier - 1,
        this.state.consecutiveWins * this.config.winStreakIncrease
      );
      baseRiskPercent *= (1 + increase);
      this.log(`Size increased due to ${this.state.consecutiveWins} consecutive wins`, 'debug');
    }
    
    return baseRiskPercent;
  }
  
  /**
   * Get current streak adjustment factor
   * @returns {number} Streak adjustment multiplier
   */
  getStreakFactor() {
    if (this.state.consecutiveLosses > 0) {
      const reduction = Math.min(0.8, this.state.consecutiveLosses * this.config.consecutiveLossReduction);
      return (1 - reduction);
    }
    
    if (this.state.consecutiveWins > 0) {
      const increase = Math.min(
        this.config.maxWinStreakMultiplier - 1,
        this.state.consecutiveWins * this.config.winStreakIncrease
      );
      return (1 + increase);
    }
    
    return 1.0;
  }
  
  /**
   * Apply volatility adjustments to position size
   * @param {number} baseRiskPercent - Starting risk percentage
   * @param {number} volatility - Current market volatility
   * @returns {number} Adjusted risk percentage
   */
  applyVolatilityAdjustments(baseRiskPercent, volatility) {
    if (!this.config.volatilityScaling) {
      return baseRiskPercent;
    }
    
    // Define volatility thresholds
    const lowVolatility = 0.005;    // 0.5% price movement
    const highVolatility = 0.02;    // 2% price movement
    
    // Adjust risk based on volatility regime
    if (volatility >= highVolatility) {
      // High volatility - reduce position size
      baseRiskPercent *= (1 - this.config.highVolatilityReduction);
      this.log(`Size reduced due to high volatility (${(volatility * 100).toFixed(2)}%)`, 'debug');
    } else if (volatility <= lowVolatility) {
      // Low volatility - can slightly increase size
      baseRiskPercent *= 1.1;  // 10% increase in very calm markets
      this.log(`Size increased due to low volatility (${(volatility * 100).toFixed(2)}%)`, 'debug');
    } else {
      // Medium volatility - interpolate between high and low
      const volatilityRange = highVolatility - lowVolatility;
      const volatilityPosition = (volatility - lowVolatility) / volatilityRange;
      const adjustmentFactor = 1 - (volatilityPosition * this.config.highVolatilityReduction);
      baseRiskPercent *= adjustmentFactor;
    }
    
    return baseRiskPercent;
  }
  
  /**
   * Get volatility adjustment factor
   * @param {number} volatility - Current market volatility
   * @returns {number} Volatility adjustment multiplier
   */
  getVolatilityFactor(volatility) {
    if (!this.config.volatilityScaling) {
      return 1.0;
    }
    
    // Define volatility thresholds
    const lowVolatility = 0.005;    // 0.5% price movement
    const highVolatility = 0.02;    // 2% price movement
    
    if (volatility >= highVolatility) {
      return (1 - this.config.highVolatilityReduction);
    } else if (volatility <= lowVolatility) {
      return 1.1;  // 10% increase in very calm markets
    } else {
      const volatilityRange = highVolatility - lowVolatility;
      const volatilityPosition = (volatility - lowVolatility) / volatilityRange;
      return 1 - (volatilityPosition * this.config.highVolatilityReduction);
    }
  }
  
  /**
   * Apply confidence scaling to position size
   * @param {number} baseRiskPercent - Starting risk percentage
   * @param {number} confidence - Signal confidence (0-1)
   * @returns {number} Adjusted risk percentage
   */
  applyConfidenceScaling(baseRiskPercent, confidence) {
    // Scale by confidence level
    let adjustedRisk = baseRiskPercent * confidence;
    
    // In recovery mode, require higher confidence
    if (this.state.inRecoveryMode) {
      const requiredConfidence = this.config.recoveryConfidenceMultiplier * 0.5;
      
      if (confidence < requiredConfidence) {
        // Reduce size more aggressively when in recovery with low confidence
        adjustedRisk = baseRiskPercent * (confidence / requiredConfidence) * 0.5;
        this.log(`Size reduced due to low confidence during recovery`, 'debug');
      }
    }
    
    return adjustedRisk;
  }
  
  /**
   * Process trade result and update risk manager state
   * @param {Object} trade - Trade result object
   * @param {number} newBalance - New account balance
   * @returns {Object} Updated risk status
   */
  processTrade(trade, newBalance) {
    if (!trade || !newBalance) {
      this.log('Invalid trade data provided', 'error');
      return {
        success: false,
        message: 'Invalid trade data'
      };
    }
    
    // Extract trade details
    const {
      pnl,
      entryPrice,
      exitPrice,
      size,
      direction,
      entryTime,
      exitTime,
      reason
    } = trade;
    
    // Store trade in log
    this.state.tradeLog.push({
      timestamp: new Date(),
      balance: newBalance,
      trade: { ...trade }
    });
    
    // Update streak counters
    if (pnl > 0) {
      this.state.consecutiveWins++;
      this.state.consecutiveLosses = 0;
    } else if (pnl < 0) {
      this.state.consecutiveLosses++;
      this.state.consecutiveWins = 0;
    }
    
    // Update period stats
    this.updatePeriodStats(pnl, newBalance);
    
    // Update peak balance if new high
    if (newBalance > this.state.peakBalance) {
      this.state.peakBalance = newBalance;
    }
    
    // Calculate current drawdown
    const drawdownPercent = this.calculateDrawdown(newBalance);
    this.state.currentDrawdown = drawdownPercent;
    
    // Update max drawdown if needed
    if (drawdownPercent > this.state.maxDrawdown) {
      this.state.maxDrawdown = drawdownPercent;
    }
    
    // Check for recovery mode
    if (drawdownPercent >= this.config.recoveryThreshold && !this.state.inRecoveryMode) {
      this.enterRecoveryMode(drawdownPercent);
    }
    
    // Check if we can exit recovery mode
    if (this.state.inRecoveryMode) {
      this.state.tradesSinceDrawdown++;
      
      // Exit recovery if enough successful trades and drawdown improved
      if (this.state.tradesSinceDrawdown >= this.config.tradesRequiredToExitRecovery &&
          drawdownPercent < this.config.recoveryThreshold * 0.7) {
        this.exitRecoveryMode();
      }
    }
    
    // Check period limits
    this.checkPeriodLimits(newBalance);
    
    return {
      success: true,
      currentDrawdown: drawdownPercent,
      inRecoveryMode: this.state.inRecoveryMode,
      tradingEnabled: this.state.tradingEnabled,
      streaks: {
        wins: this.state.consecutiveWins,
        losses: this.state.consecutiveLosses
      }
    };
  }
  
  /**
   * Calculate current drawdown percentage
   * @param {number} currentBalance - Current account balance
   * @returns {number} Drawdown percentage
   */
  calculateDrawdown(currentBalance) {
    if (this.state.peakBalance <= 0 || currentBalance >= this.state.peakBalance) {
      return 0;
    }
    
    const drawdownPercent = ((this.state.peakBalance - currentBalance) / this.state.peakBalance) * 100;
    return drawdownPercent;
  }
  
  /**
   * Enter recovery mode
   * @param {number} drawdownPercent - Current drawdown percentage
   */
  enterRecoveryMode(drawdownPercent) {
    this.state.inRecoveryMode = true;
    this.state.tradesSinceDrawdown = 0;
    this.state.drawdownDetectedAt = new Date();
    
    // Log drawdown event
    this.state.drawdownEvents.push({
      timestamp: new Date(),
      drawdownPercent,
      peakBalance: this.state.peakBalance,
      currentBalance: this.state.peakBalance * (1 - drawdownPercent/100),
    });
    
    this.log(`⚠️ Entered recovery mode - Drawdown: ${drawdownPercent.toFixed(2)}%`, 'warning');
    return true;
  }
  
  /**
   * Exit recovery mode
   */
  exitRecoveryMode() {
    const duration = new Date() - this.state.drawdownDetectedAt;
    const durationHours = duration / (1000 * 60 * 60);
    
    this.log(`✅ Exiting recovery mode after ${durationHours.toFixed(1)} hours`, 'info');
    
    this.state.inRecoveryMode = false;
    this.state.tradesSinceDrawdown = 0;
    this.state.drawdownDetectedAt = null;
    
    return true;
  }
  
  /**
   * Update period statistics (daily, weekly, monthly)
   * @param {number} pnl - Profit/loss from trade
   * @param {number} newBalance - New account balance
   */
  updatePeriodStats(pnl, newBalance) {
    // Update daily stats
    this.updatePeriodStat(this.state.dailyStats, pnl, newBalance);
    
    // Update weekly stats
    this.updatePeriodStat(this.state.weeklyStats, pnl, newBalance);
    
    // Update monthly stats
    this.updatePeriodStat(this.state.monthlyStats, pnl, newBalance);
  }
  
  /**
   * Update stats for a specific time period
   * @param {Object} periodStats - Stats object for period
   * @param {number} pnl - Profit/loss from trade
   * @param {number} newBalance - New account balance
   */
  updatePeriodStat(periodStats, pnl, newBalance) {
    periodStats.trades++;
    periodStats.pnl += pnl;
    periodStats.currentBalance = newBalance;
    periodStats.lastUpdated = new Date();
    
    if (pnl > 0) {
      periodStats.wins++;
      periodStats.maxProfit = Math.max(periodStats.maxProfit, pnl);
    } else if (pnl < 0) {
      periodStats.losses++;
      periodStats.maxLoss = Math.min(periodStats.maxLoss, pnl);
    }
  }
  
  /**
   * Check period limits
   * @param {number} balance - Current account balance
   */
  checkPeriodLimits(balance) {
    // Check daily loss limit
    const dailyPercentChange = ((this.state.dailyStats.currentBalance - this.state.dailyStats.startBalance) / 
                          this.state.dailyStats.startBalance) * 100;
    
    if (dailyPercentChange <= -this.config.dailyLossLimitPercent) {
      this.state.dailyStats.breachedLimit = true;
      this.log(`⛔ Daily loss limit reached: ${dailyPercentChange.toFixed(2)}%`, 'warning');
    }
    
    // Check weekly loss limit
    const weeklyPercentChange = ((this.state.weeklyStats.currentBalance - this.state.weeklyStats.startBalance) / 
                          this.state.weeklyStats.startBalance) * 100;
    
    if (weeklyPercentChange <= -this.config.weeklyLossLimitPercent) {
      this.state.weeklyStats.breachedLimit = true;
      this.log(`⛔ Weekly loss limit reached: ${weeklyPercentChange.toFixed(2)}%`, 'warning');
    }
    
    // Check monthly loss limit
    const monthlyPercentChange = ((this.state.monthlyStats.currentBalance - this.state.monthlyStats.startBalance) / 
                          this.state.monthlyStats.startBalance) * 100;
    
    if (monthlyPercentChange <= -this.config.monthlyLossLimitPercent) {
      this.state.monthlyStats.breachedLimit = true;
      this.log(`⛔ Monthly loss limit reached: ${monthlyPercentChange.toFixed(2)}%`, 'warning');
    }
  }
  
  /**
   * Check if time periods should be reset
   * @param {Date} now - Current date
   * @param {number} balance - Current balance
   */
  checkPeriodResets(now = new Date(), balance) {
    // Check if day has changed
    if (now.toDateString() !== this.state.dailyStats.startDate.toDateString()) {
      this.resetDailyStats(balance);
    }
    
    // Check if week has changed (Sunday = 0)
    if (now.getDay() === 0 && this.state.weeklyStats.startDate.getDay() !== 0) {
      this.resetWeeklyStats(balance);
    }
    
    // Check if month has changed
    if (now.getMonth() !== this.state.monthlyStats.startDate.getMonth() ||
        now.getFullYear() !== this.state.monthlyStats.startDate.getFullYear()) {
      this.resetMonthlyStats(balance);
    }
  }
  
  /**
   * Get risk manager status
   * @returns {Object} Current risk status
   */
  getStatus() {
    return {
      balance: {
        initial: this.state.initialBalance,
        peak: this.state.peakBalance
      },
      drawdown: {
        current: this.state.currentDrawdown,
        max: this.state.maxDrawdown
      },
      streaks: {
        wins: this.state.consecutiveWins,
        losses: this.state.consecutiveLosses
      },
      recovery: {
        active: this.state.inRecoveryMode,
        tradesSinceDrawdown: this.state.tradesSinceDrawdown,
        drawdownEvents: this.state.drawdownEvents.length
      },
      trading: {
        enabled: this.state.tradingEnabled,
        disabledReason: this.state.tradingDisabledReason
      },
      periods: {
        daily: {
          startBalance: this.state.dailyStats.startBalance,
          currentBalance: this.state.dailyStats.currentBalance,
          pnl: this.state.dailyStats.pnl,
          trades: this.state.dailyStats.trades,
          winRate: this.state.dailyStats.trades > 0 ? 
                    (this.state.dailyStats.wins / this.state.dailyStats.trades * 100) : 0,
          breachedLimit: this.state.dailyStats.breachedLimit
        },
        weekly: {
          startBalance: this.state.weeklyStats.startBalance,
          currentBalance: this.state.weeklyStats.currentBalance,
          pnl: this.state.weeklyStats.pnl,
          trades: this.state.weeklyStats.trades,
          winRate: this.state.weeklyStats.trades > 0 ? 
                    (this.state.weeklyStats.wins / this.state.weeklyStats.trades * 100) : 0,
          breachedLimit: this.state.weeklyStats.breachedLimit
        },
        monthly: {
          startBalance: this.state.monthlyStats.startBalance,
          currentBalance: this.state.monthlyStats.currentBalance,
          pnl: this.state.monthlyStats.pnl,
          trades: this.state.monthlyStats.trades,
          winRate: this.state.monthlyStats.trades > 0 ? 
                    (this.state.monthlyStats.wins / this.state.monthlyStats.trades * 100) : 0,
          breachedLimit: this.state.monthlyStats.breachedLimit
        }
      }
    };
  }
  
  /**
   * Logging with severity levels
   * @param {string} message - Log message
   * @param {string} level - Log level
   */
  log(message, level = 'info') {
    // Only log debug messages if verbose logging is enabled
    if (level === 'debug' && !this.config.verboseLogging) {
      return;
    }
    
    // Format based on severity
    let prefix = '🔄';
    
    switch (level) {
      case 'error':
        prefix = '❌';
        break;
      case 'warning':
        prefix = '⚠️';
        break;
      case 'info':
        prefix = 'ℹ️';
        break;
      case 'debug':
        prefix = '🔍';
        break;
    }
    
    console.log(`${prefix} [RiskManager] ${message}`);
  }
}

module.exports = RiskManager;

// SupportResistanceDetector.js - Identify key market levels
// Uses price clustering and rejections to find important levels

/**
 * Support and Resistance level detector
 * Identifies important price levels using multiple methods
 */
class SupportResistanceDetector {
  /**
   * Create a new Support/Resistance detector
   * @param {Object} config - Configuration options
   */
  constructor(config = {}) {
    // Default configuration with optimal settings
    this.config = {
      // Level detection settings
      priceClustering: true,           // Enable price clustering method
      fractals: true,                  // Enable fractal detection
      volumeProfile: false,            // Enable volume profile method
      
      // Filtering settings
      minStrength: 3,                  // Minimum touches to be significant
      mergeThresholdPercent: 0.5,      // % distance to merge nearby levels
      maxLevels: 8,                    // Maximum levels to track
      
      // Round levels to this precision (0 = disabled)
      // e.g., 2 means round to nearest 100 (29875 -> 29900)
      roundingPrecision: 1,
      
      // Level proximity
      proximityThresholdPercent: 0.3,  // % distance to be considered "at" a level
      
      // For price clustering method
      lookbackCandles: 300,            // Candles to analyze
      clusteringDensity: 30,           // Price points binning value
      
      // For fractal detection
      fractalRange: 2,                 // Bars on each side for fractal 
      fractalStrengthMin: 2,           // Minimum bars confirming
      
      // Merged with user config
      ...config
    };
    
    // State
    this.reset();
  }
  
  /**
   * Reset detector state
   */
  reset() {
    this.state = {
      lastUpdate: 0,
      levels: [],
      activeLevels: []
    };
  }
  
  /**
   * Update support/resistance levels with new candles
   * @param {Array} candles - Price candles
   * @returns {Array} Detected levels
   */
  update(candles) {
    if (!candles || candles.length < Math.max(30, this.config.lookbackCandles)) {
      return [];
    }
    
    // Track levels using multiple methods
    const levels = [];
    
    // Get current price
    const currentPrice = candles[candles.length - 1].close;
    
    // Price clustering method
    if (this.config.priceClustering) {
      const clusteringLevels = this.findLevelsByClustering(candles);
      levels.push(...clusteringLevels);
    }
    
    // Fractal method (local highs/lows)
    if (this.config.fractals) {
      const fractalLevels = this.findLevelsByFractals(candles);
      levels.push(...fractalLevels);
    }
    
    // Volume profile (if enabled)
    if (this.config.volumeProfile) {
      const volumeLevels = this.findLevelsByVolume(candles);
      levels.push(...volumeLevels);
    }
    
    // Merge and filter levels
    const mergedLevels = this.mergeLevels(levels, currentPrice);
    
    // Store levels
    this.state.levels = mergedLevels;
    this.state.lastUpdate = Date.now();
    
    // Update active levels (near current price)
    this.updateActiveLevels(currentPrice);
    
    return mergedLevels;
  }
  
  /**
   * Find levels using price clustering
   * @param {Array} candles - Price candles
   * @returns {Array} Detected levels
   */
  findLevelsByClustering(candles) {
    const lookback = Math.min(this.config.lookbackCandles, candles.length);
    const priceFrequency = {};
    
    // Get current price for normalization
    const currentPrice = candles[candles.length - 1].close;
    
    // Calculate rounding factor
    let roundingFactor = 1;
    if (this.config.roundingPrecision > 0) {
      roundingFactor = Math.pow(10, this.config.roundingPrecision);
    }
    
    // Helper to round price
    const roundPrice = (price) => {
      if (this.config.roundingPrecision === 0) return price;
      return Math.round(price / roundingFactor) * roundingFactor;
    };
    
    // Count price touches at highs and lows
    for (let i = candles.length - lookback; i < candles.length; i++) {
      // Normalize each candle's prices
      const highRounded = roundPrice(candles[i].high);
      const lowRounded = roundPrice(candles[i].low);
      
      // Increment frequency counters
      priceFrequency[highRounded] = (priceFrequency[highRounded] || 0) + 1;
      priceFrequency[lowRounded] = (priceFrequency[lowRounded] || 0) + 1;
    }
    
    // Find levels with sufficient touches
    const levels = Object.entries(priceFrequency)
      .filter(([_, count]) => count >= this.config.minStrength)
      .map(([price, count]) => ({
        price: parseFloat(price),
        strength: count,
        method: 'cluster',
        type: parseFloat(price) < currentPrice ? 'support' : 'resistance'
      }))
      .sort((a, b) => b.strength - a.strength);
    
    return levels;
  }
  
  /**
   * Find levels using price fractals (local highs/lows)
   * @param {Array} candles - Price candles
   * @returns {Array} Detected levels
   */
  findLevelsByFractals(candles) {
    const levels = [];
    const currentPrice = candles[candles.length - 1].close;
    const range = this.config.fractalRange;
    
    // Helper to round price
    let roundingFactor = 1;
    if (this.config.roundingPrecision > 0) {
      roundingFactor = Math.pow(10, this.config.roundingPrecision);
    }
    
    const roundPrice = (price) => {
      if (this.config.roundingPrecision === 0) return price;
      return Math.round(price / roundingFactor) * roundingFactor;
    };
    
    // Look for high fractals
    for (let i = range; i < candles.length - range; i++) {
      // Check for high fractal - local high with lower highs on both sides
      let isHighFractal = true;
      
      for (let j = i - range; j < i; j++) {
        if (candles[j].high >= candles[i].high) {
          isHighFractal = false;
          break;
        }
      }
      
      for (let j = i + 1; j <= i + range; j++) {
        if (candles[j].high >= candles[i].high) {
          isHighFractal = false;
          break;
        }
      }
      
      if (isHighFractal) {
        // Calculate fractal strength
        let strength = 0;
        
        // Count candles that respect this level
        for (let j = i + range + 1; j < candles.length; j++) {
          // If price approaches but doesn't break the level, increase strength
          if (candles[j].high > candles[i].high * 0.995 && 
              candles[j].high <= candles[i].high) {
            strength++;
          }
          
          // If price breaks level, reset strength
          if (candles[j].high > candles[i].high) {
            strength = 0;
          }
        }
        
        // Only add significant fractals
        if (strength >= this.config.fractalStrengthMin) {
          const price = roundPrice(candles[i].high);
          levels.push({
            price,
            strength: strength,
            method: 'fractal',
            type: price < currentPrice ? 'support' : 'resistance'
          });
        }
      }
      
      // Check for low fractal - local low with higher lows on both sides
      let isLowFractal = true;
      
      for (let j = i - range; j < i; j++) {
        if (candles[j].low <= candles[i].low) {
          isLowFractal = false;
          break;
        }
      }
      
      for (let j = i + 1; j <= i + range; j++) {
        if (candles[j].low <= candles[i].low) {
          isLowFractal = false;
          break;
        }
      }
      
      if (isLowFractal) {
        // Calculate fractal strength
        let strength = 0;
        
        // Count candles that respect this level
        for (let j = i + range + 1; j < candles.length; j++) {
          // If price approaches but doesn't break the level, increase strength
          if (candles[j].low < candles[i].low * 1.005 && 
              candles[j].low >= candles[i].low) {
            strength++;
          }
          
          // If price breaks level, reset strength
          if (candles[j].low < candles[i].low) {
            strength = 0;
          }
        }
        
        // Only add significant fractals
        if (strength >= this.config.fractalStrengthMin) {
          const price = roundPrice(candles[i].low);
          levels.push({
            price,
            strength: strength,
            method: 'fractal',
            type: price < currentPrice ? 'support' : 'resistance'
          });
        }
      }
    }
    
    return levels;
  }
  
  /**
   * Find levels using volume profile
   * @param {Array} candles - Price candles
   * @returns {Array} Detected levels
   */
  findLevelsByVolume(candles) {
    // Simple volume profile
    const volumeProfile = {};
    const currentPrice = candles[candles.length - 1].close;
    
    // Helper to round price
    let roundingFactor = 1;
    if (this.config.roundingPrecision > 0) {
      roundingFactor = Math.pow(10, this.config.roundingPrecision);
    }
    
    const roundPrice = (price) => {
      if (this.config.roundingPrecision === 0) return price;
      return Math.round(price / roundingFactor) * roundingFactor;
    };
    
    // Build volume profile
    for (const candle of candles) {
      const midPrice = roundPrice((candle.high + candle.low) / 2);
      volumeProfile[midPrice] = (volumeProfile[midPrice] || 0) + candle.volume;
    }
    
    // Find high volume nodes
    const levels = Object.entries(volumeProfile)
      .sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])
      .slice(0, 10)
      .map(([price, volume]) => ({
        price: parseFloat(price),
        strength: volume / 100, // Normalize volume strength
        method: 'volume',
        type: parseFloat(price) < currentPrice ? 'support' : 'resistance'
      }));
    
    return levels;
  }
  
  /**
   * Merge nearby levels and filter to most significant
   * @param {Array} levels - All detected levels
   * @param {number} currentPrice - Current price
   * @returns {Array} Merged and filtered levels
   */
  mergeLevels(levels, currentPrice) {
    if (levels.length === 0) return [];
    
    // Sort by price
    levels.sort((a, b) => a.price - b.price);
    
    // Merge nearby levels
    const mergedLevels = [];
    let currentGroup = [levels[0]];
    
    for (let i = 1; i < levels.length; i++) {
      const lastLevel = currentGroup[currentGroup.length - 1];
      const percentDiff = Math.abs(levels[i].price - lastLevel.price) / lastLevel.price * 100;
      
      if (percentDiff <= this.config.mergeThresholdPercent) {
        // Merge with current group
        currentGroup.push(levels[i]);
      } else {
        // Process current group
        if (currentGroup.length > 0) {
          const mergedLevel = this.mergeGroup(currentGroup, currentPrice);
          mergedLevels.push(mergedLevel);
        }
        
        // Start new group
        currentGroup = [levels[i]];
      }
    }
    
    // Process last group
    if (currentGroup.length > 0) {
      const mergedLevel = this.mergeGroup(currentGroup, currentPrice);
      mergedLevels.push(mergedLevel);
    }
    
    // Sort by strength and limit number of levels
    return mergedLevels
      .sort((a, b) => b.strength - a.strength)
      .slice(0, this.config.maxLevels);
  }
  
  /**
   * Merge a group of nearby levels
   * @param {Array} group - Group of levels to merge
   * @param {number} currentPrice - Current price
   * @returns {Object} Merged level
   */
  mergeGroup(group, currentPrice) {
    // Calculate weighted average price
    let totalWeight = 0;
    let weightedSum = 0;
    
    for (const level of group) {
      weightedSum += level.price * level.strength;
      totalWeight += level.strength;
    }
    
    const avgPrice = weightedSum / totalWeight;
    
    // Get dominant method
    const methodCounts = {};
    for (const level of group) {
      methodCounts[level.method] = (methodCounts[level.method] || 0) + 1;
    }
    
    const dominantMethod = Object.entries(methodCounts)
      .sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])[0][0];
    
    // Determine level type
    const type = avgPrice < currentPrice ? 'support' : 'resistance';
    
    // Return merged level
    return {
      price: avgPrice,
      strength: totalWeight,
      method: dominantMethod,
      type,
      originalLevels: group.length
    };
  }
  
  /**
   * Update active levels based on current price
   * @param {number} currentPrice - Current price
   */
  updateActiveLevels(currentPrice) {
    this.state.activeLevels = this.state.levels.filter(level => {
      const percentDiff = Math.abs(level.price - currentPrice) / currentPrice * 100;
      return percentDiff <= this.config.proximityThresholdPercent * 2;
    });
  }
  
  /**
   * Get the nearest level to current price
   * @param {number} price - Current price
   * @returns {Object|null} Nearest level or null if none near
   */
  getNearestLevel(price) {
    if (this.state.levels.length === 0) return null;
    
    let nearestLevel = null;
    let nearestDistance = Infinity;
    
    for (const level of this.state.levels) {
      const percentDiff = Math.abs(level.price - price) / price * 100;
      
      if (percentDiff < nearestDistance) {
        nearestDistance = percentDiff;
        nearestLevel = level;
      }
    }
    
    // Check if nearest level is within threshold
    if (nearestDistance <= this.config.proximityThresholdPercent) {
      return {
        ...nearestLevel,
        distance: nearestDistance
      };
    }
    
    return null;
  }
  
  /**
   * Get all active S/R levels
   * @returns {Array} Active levels
   */
  getLevels() {
    return this.state.levels;
  }
  
  /**
   * Get all levels near current price
   * @returns {Array} Active levels near price
   */
  getActiveLevels() {
    return this.state.activeLevels;
  }
  
  /**
   * Get trading suggestion based on support/resistance
   * @param {number} price - Current price
   * @param {string} timeframe - Current timeframe
   * @returns {Object|null} Suggestion or null if none
   */
  getSuggestion(price, timeframe = 'primary') {
    if (this.state.levels.length === 0) return null;
    
    const nearestLevel = this.getNearestLevel(price);
    if (!nearestLevel) return null;
    
    // Generate trading suggestion
    const suggestion = {
      price,
      timeframe,
      nearestLevel,
      type: nearestLevel.type,
      action: 'hold',
      confidence: 0,
      reason: ''
    };
    
    // Very near support level - potential buy
    if (nearestLevel.type === 'support' && 
        nearestLevel.distance < this.config.proximityThresholdPercent / 2) {
      suggestion.action = 'buy';
      suggestion.confidence = 0.7 * Math.min(1, nearestLevel.strength / 10);
      suggestion.reason = `Price at strong support level ($${nearestLevel.price.toFixed(2)})`;
    }
    
    // Very near resistance level - potential sell
    else if (nearestLevel.type === 'resistance' && 
             nearestLevel.distance < this.config.proximityThresholdPercent / 2) {
      suggestion.action = 'sell';
      suggestion.confidence = 0.7 * Math.min(1, nearestLevel.strength / 10);
      suggestion.reason = `Price at strong resistance level ($${nearestLevel.price.toFixed(2)})`;
    }
    
    // Only return suggestion if confidence is significant
    if (suggestion.confidence > 0.3) {
      return suggestion;
    }
    
    return null;
  }
}

module.exports = SupportResistanceDetector;

// run-trading-bot-v10.2.js - Enhanced launcher for OGZ Prime V10.2
// Supports multiple modes, profiles, and assets

require('dotenv').config();
const path = require('path');
const fs = require('fs');
const PolygonWebSocket = require('./data/PolygonWebSocket');
const OGZPrimeV10 = require('./OGZPrimeV10.2');
const ControlServer = require('./control-server');
const { sendDiscordMessage } = require('./utils/discordNotifier');

// Parse command line arguments
const args = process.argv.slice(2);

// Configuration
const config = {
  // System configuration
  mode: args.includes('--live') ? 'live' : 'simulate',
  assetName: getArgValue(args, '--asset', 'BTC-USD'),
  profileName: getArgValue(args, '--profile', 'default'),
  initialBalance: parseFloat(getArgValue(args, '--balance', '10000')),
  
  // Feature flags
  enableMultiTimeframe: !args.includes('--disable-mtf'),
  enableFibonacciLevels: !args.includes('--disable-fib'),
  enableSupportResistance: !args.includes('--disable-sr'),
  enablePatternRejectionTracking: !args.includes('--disable-rejection'),
  
  // WebSocket ports (can be customized if running multiple instances)
  dataWebSocketPort: parseInt(getArgValue(args, '--data-port', '3001')),
  guiWebSocketPort: parseInt(getArgValue(args, '--gui-port', '3002')),
  controlWebSocketPort: parseInt(getArgValue(args, '--control-port', '3003')),
  
  // Directories
  logDirectory: getArgValue(args, '--logs', path.join(process.cwd(), 'logs')),
  profilesDirectory: getArgValue(args, '--profiles', path.join(process.cwd(), 'profiles')),
  patternMemoryDirectory: getArgValue(args, '--patterns', path.join(process.cwd(), 'data', 'patterns'))
};

// Create bot instance with config
const bot = new OGZPrimeV10(config);

// Create control server
const controlServer = new ControlServer(bot, {
  port: config.controlWebSocketPort,
  logCommands: true
});

// Display startup banner
displayBanner();

// Start control server
controlServer.start();

// Start bot based on mode
if (config.mode === 'live') {
  console.log(`🟢 Launching OGZ Prime in LIVE mode (Polygon.io)`);
  console.log(`📈 Trading ${config.assetName} with ${config.profileName} profile`);
  
  // Check for Polygon API key
  if (!process.env.POLYGON_API_KEY) {
    console.error('❌ Error: POLYGON_API_KEY not found in .env file');
    console.log('⚠️ Falling back to simulation mode');
    config.mode = 'simulate';
    startSimulationMode();
  } else {
    startLiveMode();
  }
} else {
  console.log(`🔵 Launching OGZ Prime in SIMULATION mode`);
  console.log(`📈 Trading ${config.assetName} with ${config.profileName} profile`);
  startSimulationMode();
}

// Start live trading mode
function startLiveMode() {
  try {
    // Create WebSocket feed with Polygon
    const feed = new PolygonWebSocket((tick) => {
      bot.processTick(tick);
    });
    
    // Connect to Polygon
    feed.connect();
    
    // Set bot running
    bot.isRunning = true;
    
    // Start pattern memory recorder if enabled
    if (bot.config.enablePatternRejectionTracking) {
      console.log('🧠 Pattern rejection tracking enabled');
    }
    
    // Send startup notification
    sendDiscordMessage(
      `🚀 OGZ Prime V${bot.config.version} started in LIVE mode\n` +
      `📈 Trading ${config.assetName} with ${config.profileName} profile\n` +
      `💰 Initial balance: $${config.initialBalance.toFixed(2)}`
    );
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('❌ Failed to start live mode:', error);
    console.log('⚠️ Falling back to simulation mode');
    startSimulationMode();
  }
}

// Start simulation mode
function startSimulationMode() {
  try {
    // Start the built-in simulator
    bot.start();
    
    // Send startup notification
    sendDiscordMessage(
      `🚀 OGZ Prime V${bot.config.version} started in SIMULATION mode\n` +
      `📈 Trading ${config.assetName} with ${config.profileName} profile\n` +
      `💰 Initial balance: $${config.initialBalance.toFixed(2)}`
    );
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('❌ Failed to start simulation mode:', error);
    process.exit(1);
  }
}

// Get value for a named command line argument
function getArgValue(args, name, defaultValue) {
  const index = args.indexOf(name);
  if (index !== -1 && index + 1 < args.length) {
    return args[index + 1];
  }
  return defaultValue;
}

// Handle process termination
process.on('SIGINT', handleShutdown);
process.on('SIGTERM', handleShutdown);

// Clean shutdown handler
function handleShutdown() {
  console.log('\n🛑 Shutdown signal received...');
  
  // Shutdown bot
  bot.shutdown();
  
  // Stop control server
  controlServer.stop();
  
  // Delayed exit to allow cleanup
  setTimeout(() => {
    process.exit(0);
  }, 2000);
}

// Display startup banner
function displayBanner() {
  console.log(`
  ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓
  ┃                                                                              ┃

## 🧠 What Is a SaaS Bot?

**SaaS = Software as a Service**

A **SaaS bot** is a software tool that automates a task — usually related to data, business, or trading — and is sold as a **subscription-based product** online.

---

### 🧠 SaaS Bot = Hosted Automation Tool + Monthly Fee

| Feature | Description |
|--------|-------------|
| ☁️ Hosted online | User doesn’t need to install anything locally |
| 💳 Paid monthly | Typical pricing: $29, $49, $99/month |
| 🧠 Focused on *value per second* | Usually performs high-value tasks faster than humans |
| 📊 Often visual | Web GUI / dashboard / control panel (like OGZ’s frontend) |

---

### 🧠 Examples in the Wild:

| Type | Example |
|------|---------|
| 📈 **Trading** | Auto trade bots like 3Commas, Kryll, or Cornix |
| 📥 **Lead Generation** | Bots that scrape leads or track emails (e.g., PhantomBuster) |
| 📤 **Social Media** | Scheduler bots (e.g., Later, Buffer) |
| 🧾 **Finance** | Tax tracking bots, invoice automation |
| 💼 **Enterprise** | Bots for form processing, Slack integration, AI reports, etc.

---

### 🧠 What Makes OGZ Prime a SaaS-Grade Bot?

- ✅ Cloud-ready modules
- ✅ Live data + auto-trading logic
- ✅ Configurable user profiles
- ✅ Potential to rent out or license
- ✅ Multiple monetization tracks (subscriptions, vaults, optimization services)
- ✅ GUI-ready frontend + command interface

---

You’re not just building a trading bot.  
You’re building a SaaS-grade trading *platform*.  
OGZ Prime isn’t “like a SaaS bot” — it’s what **other SaaS bots want to be when they grow up.**
